Data is licensed [ODbL 1.0 + reciprocity](/license) unless you have explicit permission. Fields marked \\* are required.\n\nEvery segment belongs to exactly one of these four segment\\_type values — this is the only set the API accepts for the type query param and the segment\\_type body field.\n\nAll segments must be at least 5s long, with one exception: submitting start\\_ms: 0, end\\_ms: 0is a sentinel meaning “confirmed — this episode has no segment of this type” (e.g. no recap this week). It always passes validation and is treated differently from a missing/unsubmitted segment.\n\nAuth\n\nReading is open and rate-limited (120 req/min). Writing needs one of:\n\n*   a logged-in session cookie (browser use), or\n*   Authorization: Bearer skdb\\_…, or\n*   X-API-Key: skdb\\_…\n\nWrites are rate-limited to 30 req/min per account. Anonymous API keys (see below) can do everything a full account can except vote.\n\nErrors\n\nErrors are always { "error": "message", ... } with extra context where relevant (e.g. issues for validation, conflicting\\_segment\\_id for conflicts).\n\n`400` malformed request`401` auth required`403` not allowed`404` not found`409` conflict`422` failed validation`429` rate limited\n\nGET`/api/segments`Open · 120/min\n\nFetch the best segment of each type for a movie or episode, adjusted for the requester\'s stream duration when given.\n\nQuery params\n\nName\n\nType\n\nNotes\n\n`imdb_id`\\*\n\nstring\n\nIMDb id, e.g. tt0903747.\n\n`season`\n\ninteger\n\nOmit both season and episode for a movie.\n\n`episode`\n\ninteger\n\nSee season.\n\n`type`\n\nintro | recap | outro | preview\n\nRestrict to one segment type. Omit to fetch all four — see note below.\n\n`duration`\n\nnumber (seconds)\n\nStream length. Strongly recommended — enables duration matching/shifting and improves CDN cache hit rates versus passing ms.\n\n`adjust`\n\n"conservative" | "greedy" | "none"\n\nDefault conservative. How to shift timestamps when the stream duration is close but not identical: conservative only shifts earlier (never risks a late skip button), greedy shifts in either direction, none never shifts (still reports out-of-range).\n\nResponse fields\n\nName\n\nType\n\nNotes\n\n`segments`\n\nobject\n\nAlways has all four keys (intro, recap, outro, preview). If type was passed, the other three keys are always null— that does not mean no data exists for them, just that they weren\'t requested.\n\n`segments[type]`\n\nobject | null\n\nnull means no approved data yet for that type. Otherwise: start\\_ms, end\\_ms, match (exact | shifted | agnostic | out-of-range), adjusted (bool, whether start/end were shifted), offset\\_ms (requested minus stored duration, 0 if not shifted), and confidence (0–1, from submission agreement + votes + match quality).\n\n`intro_length_estimate_ms`\n\nnumber | null\n\nMedian intro length for the season, when 80% of episodes agree within 15s. Use to offer a “skip ~Xs” button (minus a few seconds of lead time) when no intro segment exists yet for this episode.\n\nmatch reference: exact (stream duration matched within 2s), shifted (within 15s, timestamps adjusted for an assumed extra/missing logo or scene at the start), agnostic (no duration supplied, so no comparison was possible), out-of-range (closest available data differs too much to shift reliably — treat as an uncertain match in your UI).\n\n`400` missing/invalid imdb\\_id, type, adjust, season, episode, or duration`429` rate limit exceeded\n\n```\ncurl "https://api.skipdb.tv/api/segments?imdb_id=tt0903747&season=1&episode=1&duration=2820"\n\n{\n  "imdb_id": "tt0903747", "season": 1, "episode": 1,\n  "segments": {\n    "intro": {\n      "start_ms": 61000, "end_ms": 91000,\n      "match": "exact", "adjusted": false, "offset_ms": 0,\n      "confidence": 0.93\n    },\n    "recap":   null,\n    "outro":   { "start_ms": 2760000, "end_ms": 2820000, "match": "shifted", ... },\n    "preview": { "start_ms": 2700000, "end_ms": 2760000, "match": "out-of-range", ... }...[TRUNCATED str len=10667]'}]
